IT SEEMS TO ME THAT I HAVE HAD ENOUGH OF THIS FASHIONED LIFE AND I WISH I COULD GO BACK TO THE OLD DAYS WHEN I WAS A LITTLE GIRL AND PLAYED IN THE GARDEN AND DIDN'T HAVE TO WORRY ABOUT SO MANY THINGS AS I DO NOW
BUT HE DIDN'T SAY ANYTHING FOR A WHILE AND WHEN HE DID SAY SOMETHING IT WAS IN A FROWNED FACE AND HIS EYES LOOKED AS IF THEY HAD SEEN SOMETHING THAT NO ONE ELSE COULD UNDERSTAND AND THEN HE SAID
IT IS NOT A MATTER OF FORTUNE BUT OF THE WILL OF THE GODS AND OF THE COURAGE OF THE HUMAN SPIRIT THAT WE SHOULD BE ABLE TO CONQUER THESE OBSTACLES AND OVERCOME THE DIFFICULTIES OF LIFE AND THAT WE SHOULD NOT BE AFRAID OF THE PAIN AND THE SUFFERING THAT COME WITH IT
BUT I DARE SAY TO YOU MY DEAR LADY THAT I WILL NOT BE SWAYED FROM MY DUTY BY ANY FEAR OF DISAPPOINTMENT
AND SO ON AND SO FORTH AND SO ON AND ON AND SO ON
HE WOULD NOT BE SILENCED BY THE WHISPERED WORDS OF THE LITTLE GIRL WHO HAD TOLD HIM THAT HE WOULD NEVER BE HAPPY AS LONG AS HE LIVED BUT HE DID NOT CARE HE WAS TOO BUSY THINKING ABOUT HIS FUTURE
BUT HE DIDN'T TELL HER THAT HE HADN'T SEEN ANY OF HIS FRIENDS SINCE HE HAD BEEN IN THE CITY HE HAD SEEN A LITTLE GIRL PLAYING BY THE SIDE OF THE STREET AND SHE HAD LOOKED UP AT HIM AS IF SHE WERE TRYING TO SAY SOMETHING TO HIM BUT HE COULDN'T MAKE OUT WHAT SHE WAS SAYING
BUT HE DIDN'T SAY ANYTHING FOR A WHILE AND THEN HE LOOKED AT THE LADY WITH A CURIOUS EXPRESSION AND SAID IN A LOW VOICE THAT HE WANTED TO TALK TO HER ABOUT SOMETHING THAT HAD BEEN ON HIS MIND FOR A LONG TIME
IDENTIFY THE TEXT CORRESPONDING TO THE SPEECH WHAT DO YOU MEAN
BUT IT'S NOT JUST ABOUT THE MONEY IT'S ABOUT THE FREEDOM TO DO WHATEVER YOU WANT TO DO IT'S ABOUT BEING YOURSELF AND NOT HAVING TO CONFORM TO OTHER PEOPLE'S IDEAS OF HOW YOU SHOULD BE
I DON'T KNOW IF I'LL BE ABLE TO GET OUT OF HERE IN TIME
AND HE SAID TO HIMSELF THAT HE WOULDN'T BE ABLE TO GO ANY FARTHER THAN THIS AND SO HE TURNED HIS BACK ON HIMSELF AND WALKED AWAY BUT HE DIDN'T FORGET THE WORDS HE HAD SPOKEN TO HIMSELF
AND IT IS A FACT THAT HE IS THE ONLY ONE IN THE WORLD WHO HAS SEEN THE FINEST AND MOST BEAUTIFUL THINGS IN THE WORLD WITH HIS OWN EYES AND HAS NOT BEEN DEPRIVED OF THE JOY OF SEEING THEM WITH HIS EYES
THAT HE WOULD NOT BE SILENCED AND THAT HE HAD A RIGHT TO SAY WHAT HE THOUGHT FOR HIMSELF AND THAT HE COULD NOT BE HELD RESPONSIBLE FOR THE CONSEQUENCES OF HIS WORDS AND THAT HE WOULD PROTECT HIMSELF AGAINST ALL COMPLAINTS THAT WERE MADE AGAINST HIM
BUT I HAVE NOT BEEN ABLE TO CONVINCE MYSELF THAT IT IS NOT POSSIBLE FOR ME TO DO ANYTHING IN THE WAY I HAVE DONE AND I AM SORRY TO SAY THAT I HAVEN'T HAD THE COURAGE TO TRY ANYTHING ELSE AND I'M AFRAID I'LL NEVER BE ABLE TO DO WHAT I WISH TO DO
WHEN I WALKED INTO THE ROOM I COULDN'T HELP BUT FEEL A SENSE OF WONDER AND A LITTLE BIT OF FEAR AT THE SAME TIME I LOOKED AROUND AT THE OLD FASHIONED CHAIRS AND FURNITURE WHICH HAD BEEN IN THE HOUSE FOR CENTURIES
THERE WAS A LITTLE GARDEN IN THE BACK OF THE HOUSE AND IT WAS FILLED WITH ALL SORTS OF PLANTS AND FLOWERS AND IT WAS THE MOST BEAUTIFUL GARDEN ON THE WHOLE NEIGHBORHOOD IT WAS AS IF THE GARDEN HAD BEEN PLANTED BY THE GODS THEMSELVES
BUT HE DIDN'T SAY ANYTHING FOR A WHILE AND WHEN HE FINALLY DID SAY SOMETHING IT WAS ONLY TO SAY THAT HE WOULDN'T BE AFRAID OF ANYTHING AND THAT HE WOULD DO WHATEVER HE THOUGHT WOULD BE BEST FOR HIMSELF
THAT'S THE WAY IT IS AND THAT'S THE WAY I'M GOING TO DO IT
I DON'T KNOW IF I'LL BE ABLE TO DO IT BUT I'LL GIVE IT A SHOT AND SEE WHAT HAPPENS
SHE LOOKED AT HIM WITH EYES THAT SEEMED TO SEE RIGHT THROUGH HIM TO HIS VERY SOUL SHE SAID IN A VOICE THAT WAS LIKE THE SOUND OF THE WIND
AND I'LL GIVE YOU A HUNDRED DOLLARS IF YOU'LL COME WITH ME I'LL TAKE YOU TO MY FATHER'S PLACE AND WE'LL SIT DOWN TO EAT AND DRINK AND HAVE A GOOD TIME
AND I'LL GIVE YOU A CHANCE TO SHOW ME YOU'RE A GOOD BOY TELL ME YOU'LL BE MY FRIEND AND I'LL BE YOUR FRIEND AND WE'LL PLAY TOGETHER AND HAVE A GOOD TIME I'LL SHOW YOU WHAT I CAN DO AND YOU CAN SHOW ME WHAT YOU CAN DO
THAT I WOULD BE HAPPY TO BE A PART OF IT BUT I DON'T KNOW IF I CAN DO IT I DON'T LIKE THE IDEA OF BEING A PART OF ANYTHING THAT IS NOT MY OWN AND I DON'T WANT TO BE PART OF ANY THING THAT I DON'T UNDERSTAND I DON'T THINK I CAN UNDERSTAND ANYTHING
AT THE TIME OF HIS BIRTH HE HAD NOT YET SEEN THE LIGHT OF DAY AND HAD BEEN BORN IN THE SHADOWS OF HIS MOTHER'S WARDROBE HE HAD NEVER SEEN ANYTHING BUT DARKNESS AND DARKNESS AND HAD NEVER HEARD ANY SOUND BUT THE SOUND OF HIS OWN HEART BEATING
IT WAS THE FIRST TIME I HAD EVER SEEN SUCH A THING IN MY LIFE AND I DIDN'T KNOW WHAT TO DO WITH MYSELF IT WAS ALL SO STRANGE AND UNFAMILIAR TO ME AND I FELT AS IF I HAD LOST MY SENSE OF REALITY
BUT THE FINEST THINGS IN THE WORLD ARE NOT TO BE FOUND IN THE EARTH BUT IN THE HEARTS OF MEN AND WOMEN AND IN THE SPIRIT OF THE NATURAL WORLD AND IN THE FLOWING OF THE RIVERS AND THE GROWING OF THE PLANTS AND THE BREATHING OF THE BREEZE
WHEN I WAS A YOUNG GIRL I WENT TO THE FARM OF MY FATHER'S NEIGHBOUR HE WAS A GOOD MAN AND A KIND FATHER TO ME HE TOLD ME THAT HE WANTED ME TO GO TO SCHOOL BUT I DIDN'T WANT TO GO I WANTED TO STAY AT HOME AND PLAY WITH MY FATHER
BUT HE DIDN'T SAY ANYTHING FOR A FEW MINUTES AND THEN SAID IN A LOW VOICE THAT HE WOULDN'T SAY ANY MORE FOR THE SAKE OF HIS FRIENDS HE SAID THAT HE WOULD TELL HIM ALL ABOUT IT LATER WHEN HE HAD A FEW MORE WORDS TO SPARE
WHEN HE HAD FINALLY COME TO HIS SENSES HE WAS ASHAMED OF WHAT HE HAD SAID AND OF WHAT HE WOULD HAVE SAID IF HE HAD NOT BEEN SO TEMPTED BY THE BEAUTY OF THE LADY'S EYES AND THE SOUND OF HER VOICE HE HAD COME TO THE POINT WHERE HE COULD NOT STAND ANY LONGER
THEY WERE AS IF THEY HAD COME FROM THE DEPTHS OF THE OCEAN AND HAD SEEN ALL THE WONDERS OF THE UNIVERSE AND NOW THEY WERE GOING BACK TO THEIR OWN PLACE BUT THEY DIDN'T WANT TO GO BACK THEY WANTED TO STAY
IF HE HAD NOT BEEN A MAN OF GREAT INTELLIGENCE AND WISDOM HE WOULD NOT HAVE BEEN ABLE TO COME UP WITH SUCH A PLAN HE WOULD HAVE BEEN A FOOL WHO WOULD NOT BE ABLE TO THINK FOR HIMSELF
HE SAID THAT HE HAD NOT SEEN ANYTHING LIKE IT BEFORE AND THAT HE COULDN'T BELIEVE HIS EYES TO BE OPEN TO SUCH A THING HE SAID THAT IT MUST HAVE BEEN SOME KIND OF FANTASY
BUT HE DIDN'T HEAR THE WORDS I SAID TO HIM FOR A MOMENT AND THEN HE LOOKED AT ME WITH A CURIOUS EXPRESSION AS IF I HAD JUST SAID SOMETHING THAT HAD NEVER BEEN SAID BEFORE HE SAID IN A THOUGHTFUL MANNER
IT WAS THE FIRST TIME HE HAD EVER SEEN A WOMAN WHO DIDN'T LOOK LIKE A LADY AND HE FELT AS IF HE HAD STUMBLED UPON SOMETHING HE HAD NEVER SEEN BEFORE THE LADY LOOKED UP AT HIM WITH EYES THAT SEEMED TO SEE RIGHT THROUGH HIM
THAT'S THE WAY TO DO IT I'LL SHOW YOU HOW IT'S DONE AND I'LL TELL YOU ALL THE SECRETS THAT I'VE LEARNED FROM MY MOTHER AND MY FATHER AND MY GRANDFATHER AND SO ON AND SO ON I'LL GIVE YOU ALL THE KNOWLEDGE OF THE WORLD
BUT HE HAD NOT THOUGHT OF IT AT THE TIME AND HAD NOT SEEN IT WITH HIS OWN EYES AND SOON ENOUGH HE FOUND HIMSELF IN A VERY DIFFERENT POSITION THAN HE WOULD HAVE BEEN IF HE HAD BEEN PREPARED FOR SUCH A SITUATION AND HE COULDN'T HELP BUT FEEL A SENSE OF DISAPPOINTMENT AND FRUSTRATION
BUT HE DIDN'T SAY ANYTHING FOR A WHILE AND THEN HE LOOKED AT HER WITH A CURIOUS EXPRESSION AND SAID IN A DEEP VOICE THAT HE WANTED TO KNOW WHY SHE HAD COME TO THE ISLAND AND WHAT SHE WANTED TO DO THERE BUT SHE DIDN'T ASK HIM ANY QUESTIONS
HE HAD NOT SEEN HER SINCE THE DAY SHE HAD LEFT HIM AND HE HAD NOT THOUGHT OF HER SINCE THAT DAY HE HAD FELT AS IF HE HAD LOST HER FOREVER HE HAD THOUGHT THAT SHE WOULD NEVER COME BACK TO HIM AND THAT HE WOULD NEVER SEE HER AGAIN
WHEN I WAS A YOUNG GIRL I WENT TO THE FARM OF MY FATHER'S NEIGHBOR AND ASKED HIM IF HE WOULD BE WILLING TO LET ME HELP HIM IN HIS WORKS AND HE SAID YES HE WOULD AND I HAD TO HELP HIM IN THE FIELDS AND IN THE GARDEN
BUT HE HAD NOT TOLD HER THAT HE WAS GOING TO COME TO HIS FATHER'S HOUSE AND HE DID NOT TELL HER THAT HE WOULD COME TO HER FATHER'S AND SOON AFTER HE HAD COME TO THE HOUSE OF HIS FATHER AND HIS FATHER HAD TOLD HIM THAT HE COULD NOT COME
SHE CAME UP TO ME AND TOLD ME THAT SHE WANTED TO GO TO THE GARDEN BUT I DIDN'T WANT HER TO GO THERE BECAUSE I HAD A FEELING THAT SHE WOULD GET INTO SOME KIND OF TROUBLE IF SHE WENT THERE
BUT INSTEAD OF FLYING UP TO THE SKY LIKE A BIRD IT FLEW DOWN TO THE DEPTHS OF THE OCEAN WHERE IT CAME TO REST ON THE ROCKS OF THE MOUNTAINS AND WAS SUSTAINED BY THE POWER OF THE WAVES AND THE TIDES
THEY WERE IN A STATE OF GREAT EXCITEMENT AND FELT THAT THEY WERE ON THE VERGE OF A GREAT TRIUMPH AND A NEW ERA IN THE HISTORY OF THE HUMAN SPIRIT IT WAS AS IF THE VERY FABRIC OF THE UNIVERSE HAD BEEN REVEALED TO THEM
